Coupon time windows are independently settled by D-047. **OPEN:** code formats/uniqueness, redemption lifecycle rules (D-048 sets optional global caps; D-050 restricts MVP redemption to signed-in customers; D-051 supersedes D-049 with one use per customer per coupon; D-052 counts use at order confirmation, while pre-confirmation bounded holds are now approved by D-054 and pre-Preparing cancellation restoration is settled by D-053), other customer eligibility, activation/default/automatic promotion behavior, one-code-per-order is settled by D-061; coupon versus item/Cart/Shipping/Manual stacking and priority, calculation bases, refunds, fraud/abuse controls, and exact Category/Cart/Shipping discount formulas. It does not approve advanced professional campaigns. |
| D-047 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Flexible Coupon Validity & Scheduling in both Basic and Advanced.** A Coupon may have an optional start date/time (when omitted, it can be active immediately) and an optional end date/time (when omitted, it may remain active without a fixed expiry). Merchants can schedule a Coupon to become eligible from a future start date/time, and can manually disable a Coupon at any time. A scheduled Coupon is NOT redeemable before its configured start. | Approval covers optional validity bounds, scheduled start, indefinite validity, and manual deactivation for Coupon Codes. **D-054 approves bounded temporary holds on order placement; D-055 approves merchant selection from predefined durations, D-058 sets the choices/maximum and D-059 sets a 2-hour new-store default.** Pre-Preparing cancellation restoration is settled by D-053 (D-048 and D-051 govern MVP global and per-customer limits, D-050 governs signed-in eligibility, and D-052 counts finalized use at order confirmation); exact timezone and start/end boundary semantics; behavior on editing dates of active Coupons; redemption counting, code uniqueness, customer eligibility, stacking, and whether the same scheduling scheme applies to non-coupon automatic campaigns. No auto-renewal or additional advanced rules are implied. |
| D-048 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Optional Global Coupon Usage Limit in both Basic and Advanced (MVP).** The merchant may set a positive maximum number of total coupon redemptions (e.g. 100) or leave the global limit unset (unlimited while other eligibility and validity conditions are met). If both a scheduled expiry and a usage cap exist, the coupon ceases to admit new eligible redemptions when the earlier restriction is reached; manual disable remains available under D-047. | Approval settles ONLY whether an overall redemption cap is optional and available in both tiers. **D-052 settles when a final redemption is counted (order confirmation). OPEN:** failed-payment handling, later-stage cancellation/refund treatment, pre-confirmation holds approved by D-054, with merchant-configurable predefined durations approved by D-055 (D-058 settles choices/maximum and D-059 sets the 2-hour default; specific exception flows remain OPEN; pre-Preparing cancellation restoration is D-053), concurrent checkout handling, code reuse, editing the cap after prior redemptions, and other coupon eligibility, priority, and stacking rules. Does not imply approval of usage limits for non-coupon campaigns. |
| D-049 | SUPERSEDED FOR MVP BY D-051 (historical decision, 2026-11-14) | **Optional Per-Customer Coupon Usage Limit in BOTH Basic and Advanced (MVP).** For each coupon, the merchant may configure a maximum number of eligible redemptions **by the same customer**, such as one redemption or three, or leave the per-customer limit unset (unlimited subject to other coupon rules). This limit is independent of the optional overall coupon redemption cap approved in D-048; when both are set, both constraints must be satisfied before a redemption is permitted. | Approval covers availability and optional configuration of the per-customer limit only. **OPEN:** how the same customer is reliably recognized, especially at guest checkout; whether phone/email verification, account login or another identifier is required; when a redemption counts; concurrent orders; later-stage cancellation, failed-payment and refund treatment (pre-Preparing cancellation restoration is D-053); applicability to orders made manually; customer merges/contact changes; privacy and anti-abuse policies. It did not establish a customer-identity model; **the optional per-customer limit policy in this historical decision is now replaced in MVP by D-051 (fixed one redemption per registered customer per coupon).** |


| D-050 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) / FUTURE GUEST OPTION DEFERRED | **Registered Customers Only for Coupon Redemption in MVP.** In both Basic and Advanced, a coupon can be redeemed only by a customer signed in to a registered account for the relevant merchant store. Guest checkout may remain available for non-coupon orders, but a guest is **not eligible to redeem any coupon in MVP**. The merchant's desired **future settings option** to allow coupon redemption by unregistered/guest customers is explicitly **DEFERRED**, not a current setting or promised MVP behavior. | This supersedes the earlier OPEN choice between guest verification/phone matching and registered-only coupon eligibility for MVP. The future merchant-side guest setting is a direction for later discovery only: guest identification/abuse prevention, verification, rollout and applicability to specific coupons are NOT defined. Account signup/login UX and whether guest can convert an existing order are not decided. Customer identity is scoped to a merchant's store; no cross-tenant identity sharing is assumed. |
| D-051 | APPROVED (2026-11-14), SUPERSEDES D-049 FOR MVP | **Fixed Once Per Registered Customer Per Coupon in MVP.** Every coupon in Basic or Advanced may be successfully redeemed **at most once by each registered customer account for that specific coupon**. There is no merchant-configurable per-customer 2/3/unlimited option in MVP; this replaces D-049's earlier optional per-customer cap, without deleting its historical record. **Global Coupon Usage Limit remains optional and merchant-controlled (D-048)**: a merchant may set a total cap on that one coupon's redemptions across all eligible customers in that store or leave it unlimited; the actual overall usage count is tracked for that coupon. If the global cap is set, both the per-customer once-only rule and overall cap must be satisfied. | **Global means total per coupon within its merchant/Tenant**, NOT across different merchants or all coupons on the platform. The merchant controls the overall cap, not arbitrary editing of counted redemptions. **D-052 settles counting at order confirmation. D-054 approves bounded holds at placement. OPEN:** exact hold duration, concurrent processing details, what happens after cancellation once Preparing has started, failed payment or refund (pre-Preparing cancellation restoration is settled by D-053), and manual/assisted order channel applicability. No special fraud strategy or cross-coupon one-use restriction is implied. |
| D-052 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Coupon redemption is counted at ORDER CONFIRMATION (MVP).** Applying/entering a Coupon on an order that has not been confirmed does NOT finalize a counted redemption. On successful order confirmation, count one redemption for that Coupon against its merchant-controlled optional global cap (D-048) and against the once-per-registered-customer-per-coupon rule (D-051). This applies to all eligible orders regardless of payment method, including **Cash on Delivery (COD)**: do not wait for cash collection. Under automatic confirmation, count at the automatic confirmation event; under manual confirmation, count when the order is confirmed. | This approves the **final counted redemption milestone only**. **D-054 settles bounded coupon holds at order placement; D-055 approves merchant-selected preset durations, D-058 defines the options/maximum, and D-059 sets the default to 2 hours.** Expired-hold confirmation outcomes are addressed by D-056/D-057. **OPEN:** exact concurrent-checkout mechanics, exactly-once behavior under retries, failed/abandoned orders, post-confirmation cancellation/return cases beyond the pre-Preparing restoration approved in D-053, and detailed interactions with payment failures. No automatic restoration policy is implied. |

| D-053 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Restore Coupon Redemption on Order Cancellation BEFORE Preparing Begins (MVP).** A coupon redemption finalized at order confirmation under D-052 is restored if the confirmed order is subsequently CANCELLED before the Preparing stage has started. Restore both (1) one redemption slot to the coupon's per-merchant global use count/remaining capacity under D-048 and (2) the registered customer's eligibility to use that same coupon again under D-051. Restoration must be recorded against the original order and performed at most once even if cancellation handling is repeated. At the next attempted use, all current coupon restrictions still apply: active status, validity dates and any global cap. | Once Preparing has begun, there is **NO AUTOMATIC restoration** merely because the order is later cancelled; likewise no automatic re-use entitlement after shipment/delivery or a return. Handling of delivery failures, RTO, refund exceptions or discretionary manual overrides remains OPEN rather than implicitly granting a restore. A coupon on an unconfirmed order has no finalized redemption to restore; D-054 approves temporary holds at order placement and D-055 makes the duration merchant-selectable from presets; near-cap processing details remain OPEN (D-058 approves the four presets/maximum and D-059 approves the 2-hour default). This policy is a business rule, not an authorization to modify order-cancellation permissions or post-Preparing lifecycle rules. |

| D-054 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Temporary Coupon Hold at ORDER PLACEMENT, with a bounded duration (MVP).** Entering or applying a coupon in the shopping cart/checkout without submitting an order does **not** reserve redemption capacity. When an eligible registered customer places an order using a coupon, provisionally HOLD the coupon use against any global cap (D-048) and that customer's once-per-coupon entitlement (D-051), so multiple pending orders cannot oversubscribe the last available redemption. On order confirmation, a still-valid hold is converted into the one finalized redemption counted under D-052; on order rejection or hold expiry, release the hold rather than counting a final use. The policy applies under automatic and merchant-manual order confirmation; a pending order does not hold capacity forever. | **D-055 resolves merchant configurability using predefined duration choices, D-058 sets options/maximum, and D-059 sets a 2-hour default. OPEN:** expiry-triggered release timing; automatic revalidation when the coupon remains available after an expired hold is APPROVED in D-057 (D-056 governs if unavailable); payment failure/abandonment handling, extension/renewal rules, concurrent processing mechanics, and whether an alternative coupon or customer re-consent is required if the offer is no longer available. The customer's agreed order total must NOT be silently increased when a hold expires. This is a coupon-capacity hold, not an inventory reservation. D-053 remains the separate rule for restoring an already finalized use if a confirmed order is cancelled before Preparing. |

| D-055 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Merchant-Configurable Temporary Coupon Hold Duration (MVP).** Under D-054, for orders pending manual merchant confirmation, the merchant may select the coupon temporary-hold lifetime **from a bounded list of predefined duration options** in that store's settings. It is NOT a single universal fixed hold duration for all tenants. The setting applies within both Basic and Advanced Coupon capabilities; the Platform Super Admin still controls feature entitlements, while authorized merchant users configure the available hold duration. With automatic confirmation, holds ordinarily convert immediately on confirmation, but the D-054 capacity-safety rule still applies. | **D-059 approves a 2-hour default for new merchants and D-060 makes later changes future-order-only** (D-058 approves the four exact presets and 24-hour maximum). **OPEN:** whether staff may change this setting, extension/renewal policy, automatic revalidation when a coupon remains available is settled by D-057; precise notification and customer consent mechanics, payment failures, and precise event/revalidation timing. D-054 remains the rule for creating, converting and releasing holds; D-052 still counts final redemption only at order confirmation. No coupon hold changes inventory reservations. |
| D-056 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Merchant Review When a Temporary Coupon Hold Expires and the Coupon Is No Longer Available (MVP).** If a manually pending order's temporary coupon hold expires (D-054/D-055) and the coupon is no longer available/eligible at confirmation time, do **not** silently reprice or automatically cancel the order. Keep the order pending merchant review, and prevent confirmation using that unavailable coupon. An authorized merchant user may (a) honor the originally agreed discounted total using a separately permitted **Manual Order Discount** (D-043/D-044), or (b) seek the customer's explicit approval of a revised order total before confirming it. The original quoted total must be visible to the merchant for review. | The merchant review and explicit customer consent principles are approved, **not** a particular consent channel, timeout, workflow status name, notification, staff permission design, handling of orders already paid, or ability to force coupon redemption beyond its cap/expiry. Handling an expired hold when the coupon **is still valid and capacity remains** is settled by D-057 (automatic revalidation at merchant confirmation). Hold extension/renewal and payment adjustments remain OPEN; D-060 settles that merchant duration changes apply to future orders only (duration presets and maximum approved by D-058; 2-hour default approved by D-059). D-052 (final use at confirmation) and D-053 (restoration on cancellation before Preparing) remain unchanged. |
| D-057 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Automatic Coupon Revalidation at Merchant Confirmation After an Expired Temporary Hold (MVP).** If a pending manually confirmed order's temporary Coupon hold has expired, then at the merchant's confirmation attempt, automatically recheck that the coupon remains active, is within its validity window, meets applicable eligibility conditions, has available global capacity (D-048), and is still usable by that registered customer (D-050/D-051). If all checks pass, honor the coupon on that pending order and finalize one coupon redemption at confirmation (D-052), without forcing a separate manual coupon-review step or changing the customer's original discounted total. This confirmation must not admit more redemptions than the limit permits, including concurrent confirmations. If revalidation fails, route to the **Merchant Review** outcome approved in D-056 instead; never silently reprice, force an invalid coupon, or automatically cancel. | Approval is for automatic coupon revalidation at confirmation after an expired hold **when still valid and capacity is available**, not for automatically extending holds or guaranteeing a coupon after expiry. **D-059 sets the 2-hour default and D-060 applies later preset changes only to future orders** (D-058 sets the choices and 24-hour maximum). **OPEN:** precise revalidation notification wording/UI, other order eligibility/payment complications, retries/extension, and technical transaction mechanisms. D-056 still governs coupon unavailable at confirmation. No change to inventory reservation rules. |


| D-058 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **MVP Temporary Coupon Hold Duration Presets:** Under D-054 and D-055, merchants may choose from EXACTLY **30 minutes, 2 hours, 12 hours, or 24 hours** for the temporary coupon redemption-capacity hold of a placed order awaiting merchant confirmation. The **maximum permitted hold is 24 hours**. The selected preset is a merchant store setting; this is NOT a platform-global fixed lifetime. | The four allowed choices and the 24-hour maximum are APPROVED. **D-059 settles the initial/default selection for new merchants at 2 hours; D-060 settles future-only changes. OPEN:** staff-setting permissions, notification details, expiry execution, payment-failure corner cases, and renewal/extension policy. D-052/D-054/D-056/D-057 continue to govern counting, holding, expiry/revalidation and merchant review. This setting does not reserve stock. |
| D-059 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Default Coupon Hold Duration for New Stores = 2 Hours (MVP).** Upon creation of a new merchant store, its temporary Coupon redemption hold duration setting defaults to **2 hours**, one of the four approved presets in D-058 (30 minutes, 2 hours, 12 hours, 24 hours; maximum 24 hours). The authorized merchant may subsequently select another approved preset, per D-055. This default is the store-setting initial value, not a fixed duration across all tenants. | Approval settles ONLY the default value for new stores; D-060 settles future-order-only setting changes. **OPEN:** editing permissions, audit requirements, changes during a hold, hold extension/renewal, expiry processing and notifications, and payment failure behavior. No changes to D-052 (counting upon confirmation), D-054 (hold from order placement), D-056/D-057 (expiry outcomes), D-053 (pre-Preparing cancellation restoration), or inventory reservation. |
| D-060 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Changes to Merchant Coupon Hold Duration Apply to Future Orders Only (MVP).** When the merchant changes the store-level coupon temporary-hold duration preset (D-055/D-058/D-059), the new value applies only to coupon holds created for orders PLACED AFTER that change. Existing holds on already placed/pending orders retain the exact expiry time set when those holds were created; they must not be automatically shortened or extended by changing the store setting. Example: an order placed at 10:00 with a 2-hour hold still expires at 12:00, even when the merchant changes the store preset to 24 hours at 10:30. | The decision settles **non-retroactive settings changes** only. It does NOT approve extending an individual existing hold, manual hold renewal, editing staff permissions, notifications or technical timers. D-054 governs hold creation and release, D-052 final count at confirmation, and D-056/D-057 govern expired-hold outcomes. This does not affect inventory reservations. |
| D-061 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **One Coupon Code Per Order in MVP (Basic and Advanced).** A customer may redeem AT MOST ONE coupon code on the same order, irrespective of the store's Basic or Advanced Discount entitlement. Multiple different coupon codes may NOT be entered/combined in a single order; a customer can select a different single code instead, without combining the codes. Multiple-coupon redemption is DEFERRED beyond MVP. | This limits **coupon-code count on one order only**. It does NOT prohibit a single coupon from combining with eligible automatic Product/Variant/Category/Cart/Shipping promotions or authorized Manual Order Discounts; D-062 now settles merchant-controlled coupon-versus-automatic stacking (default OFF) but detailed selection/priority, calculation and Manual Order Discount interaction remain OPEN. D-042, D-044, and D-045 remain authoritative for their independently approved item-level/manual/Cart stacking cases. Coupon eligibility, counts, temporary holds and cancellation restoration remain governed by D-048, D-050 to D-054 and D-056 to D-060. No multiple-coupon features or limit other than one code are approved for MVP. |
| D-062 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Merchant-Controlled Stacking: One Coupon with Automatic Promotions (Basic + Advanced MVP).** For an eligible single coupon code (D-061), the merchant may explicitly choose whether it may combine with otherwise eligible automatic promotions within the tenant's entitled scope (D-036/D-037/D-046). **Default: combination is OFF**; do not stack the coupon with automatic discounts by default. Combination is allowed only when deliberately enabled and compatible with existing business guardrails. Competing item-level Product/Variant/Category discounts still obey **Best Discount Wins on the same unit** under D-042 and must not be added together merely because coupon stacking is enabled. This is separate from the manual-order discount policy (D-044) and automatic Cart-campaign setting (D-045). | This approves merchant control and default only, NOT a particular UI configuration location, a new right to use multiple coupon codes, stacking two item discounts on one unit, or a complete discount-selection algorithm. **OPEN:** which discount prevails when coupon-vs-automatic stacking is disabled (best entire order vs explicit coupon priority vs automatic priority), precise ordering/calculation across Product/Category/Cart/Shipping, interactions with Manual Order Discounts, combining with a non-combinable Cart campaign, multiple automatic Cart promotions, coupon redemption/hold treatment for unused coupons is settled by D-064; taxes/refunds and tie-breaking remain OPEN. Shopify documents configurable combinations and best eligible discount if incompatible (https://help.shopify.com/en/manual/discounts/discount-combinations); Salla documents a merchant option to combine a promotional offer with a coupon (https://help.salla.sa/article/-%D8%A5%D8%B6%D8%A7%D9%81%D8%A9-%D8%B9%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%AE%D8%A7%D8%B5-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF/okwxus4xcmvkz359dwhu0ucc). Benchmarks are not adopted automatically. |
| D-063 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Best Total Discount Wins when a Coupon and Automatic Promotions cannot stack.** If an eligible customer-entered Coupon conflicts with an eligible automatic promotion or non-combinable automatic discount set, because combining is disabled under D-062, choose the eligible alternative that gives the customer the **largest actual total monetary saving on that order**, instead of giving unconditional priority to the Coupon or automatic offer. Clearly inform the customer when the entered Coupon is not applied because an automatic offer gives greater savings. Example: pre-discount item total 800 EGP; automatic promotion saves 100 EGP (total 700) versus non-combinable 15% Cart Coupon saving 120 EGP (total 680): apply the Coupon, resulting in 680 EGP before any unsettled taxes or shipping treatment. | Approval covers **the no-stacking Coupon-versus-automatic comparison and customer-facing explanation**, not a right to stack incompatible discounts. D-042 remains authoritative for competing item-level discounts; D-045 for optional Cart-versus-item stacking; D-044 for manual discounts; D-061 for only one Coupon per order. **D-064 closes unused Coupon holds/consumption**; **D-065 resolves Coupon-versus-automatic equal-saving ties**; **OPEN:** exactly how eligible multi-discount combinations are evaluated; detailed calculation/rounding/order for item, Cart, Shipping, tax and manual adjustments; coupon interactions with refund allocation. No new coupon-redemption counting policy is implied. |

| D-064 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Count Only Coupons Actually Applied (No Hold or Redemption for Losing/Unused Codes).** When a registered customer enters an otherwise eligible Coupon, but the system selects a more beneficial non-combinable automatic promotion under D-063 and therefore does **not actually apply** the Coupon to the order, that Coupon MUST NOT reserve any temporary redemption capacity at order placement, MUST NOT consume any global redemption slot at order confirmation, and MUST NOT consume the customer's single permitted use of that code (D-051). It remains usable in another eligible order while valid and within its limits. D-054 holds and D-052 final redemption counting apply only to Coupon savings actually applied to the order. Example: Coupon SAVE10 would save 50 EGP, but an automatic offer saves 100 EGP; apply the automatic offer and leave Coupon counters and customer eligibility untouched. | Approval covers **only non-applied Coupons losing to a better promotion**. D-065 resolves equal-saving Coupon-versus-automatic ties; it does not decide how to react if discount winners change after order edits or before confirmation, technical concurrency controls, post-confirmation adjustments, or accounting for coupon eligibility after re-pricing. No exception to D-061 (only one Coupon per order). |

| D-065 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Automatic Promotion Wins When a Coupon and Non-Stackable Automatic Discount Save Exactly the Same Total Amount.** When D-062 prohibits combination and D-063 compares an entered Coupon with a competing eligible automatic promotion or automatic discount set, if their actual total monetary savings for that order are **equal**, apply the **automatic** offer. The entered Coupon is **NOT actually applied**, and therefore per D-064 it neither reserves a temporary use nor consumes a finalized global/customer use. Communicate that the customer received the same savings automatically and the coupon remains unused. Example: order amount 1,000 EGP; Coupon saves 100 EGP and automatic promotion saves 100 EGP; apply automatic promotion, final illustrative amount 900 EGP before unsettled taxes/shipping. | This tie-breaker is limited to **Coupon versus incompatible automatic promotion(s) when total order savings are equal**. It does NOT decide ties between two competing item-level promotions, ties between multiple automatic Cart campaigns, detailed discount calculation order, discount allocation, taxes/shipping, refunds, or the outcome when a coupon and automatic discounts are allowed to stack. D-061 still limits one Coupon code per order; D-064 protects a non-applied Coupon from usage counters. |


| D-066 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Calculation Basis and Sequence When Item-Level and Cart Discounts Are Explicitly Allowed to Stack (MVP).** First calculate eligible Product, Variant and Category item-level savings, using the single best eligible discount per unit under D-042. Sum the item amounts **after those item-level discounts**. When an eligible Advanced Cart-level discount is allowed to combine with those item discounts under D-045 (and where a Coupon activates such a Cart discount, under D-062), calculate the Cart discount from this **remaining discounted merchandise subtotal**, not from the original pre-item-discount subtotal. Example: merchandise 800 EGP, item-level savings 100 EGP, remaining items subtotal 700 EGP, Cart discount 10% of 700 = 70 EGP, illustrative remaining amount 630 EGP, before unresolved shipping/tax treatment. Catalog Product/Variant selling prices are unchanged. | This approval fixes ONLY the Cart discount's basis/ordering relative to eligible item-level discounts **when combining is permitted**, not a blanket permission to stack. **OPEN:** Manual Order Discount interactions/position relative to Cart/Coupon campaigns (D-067 separately settles manual percentage-after-item basis only), shipping discounts and shipment-fee calculations, taxes, fixed-amount Cart discount limits, multiple Cart campaigns, discount-application eligibility and rounding, coupon-specific exclusion conditions, refund/proration, historical pricing snapshots, and what wins when a Cart campaign's stacking is disabled. D-044, D-045, D-062 and D-063 remain authoritative for whether particular discounts may combine. |

| D-067 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Percentage Manual Order Discount Uses Remaining Merchandise Amount After Item-Level Discounts When Explicitly Stacked (MVP).** Where a merchant/authorized actor explicitly chooses ADD rather than REPLACE under D-044, calculate the one-off manual **percentage** discount against the **remaining merchandise amount after the applicable Product/Variant/Category item-level discounts**, not the pre-discount merchandise subtotal. Example: items 1,000 EGP; item-level discounts 100 EGP; remaining 900 EGP; explicitly stacked manual 10% = 90 EGP; illustrative resulting merchandise amount 810 EGP, before unresolved shipping/tax effects. Original catalog prices remain unchanged. | Approval settles ONLY the **percentage** calculation base when a manual order discount is explicitly stacked with item-level promotions. It DOES NOT automatically permit combining a Manual Order Discount with an Advanced Cart campaign, Coupon, or Shipping offer, or determine the order/basis if multiple order-level discounts coexist. Fixed-amount Manual Order Discount capping/eligibility, staff permission details, required reasons/audit, timing in order lifecycle, tax/shipping treatment, discounts proration/refunds and rounding remain OPEN. D-043/D-044/D-066 remain authoritative in their respective scopes. |

| D-068 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Merchant-Controlled Stacking of Manual Order Discounts with Advanced Automated Cart Discount Campaigns (MVP).** When an order is eligible for an existing Advanced automated Cart campaign and the merchant/authorized staff wants to add a one-off Manual Order Discount, the DEFAULT is **NO STACKING**. The merchant/authorized staff explicitly chooses either to retain the Cart campaign discount, replace that Cart campaign discount with the Manual Order Discount, or combine both discounts for that particular order. Combining is NEVER automatic. An order-specific choice does not modify the underlying Cart campaign, the tenant's feature entitlements, or catalog Product/Variant selling prices. Example: 1,000 EGP items with automatic Cart discount 10% (100 EGP) and a fixed manual order discount 50 EGP yield 900 EGP if only the campaign applies, 950 EGP if only the manual discount applies, and 850 EGP if stacking is explicitly selected, before unsettled taxes/shipping. | Approval settles whether Manual and automated Cart order-level discounts may coexist and the explicit opt-in/default choice ONLY. **D-071 now settles Cart-first, then Manual Discount on the remaining amount when their combination is explicitly allowed. OPEN:** relationships with a Coupon, interaction with additional simultaneous campaigns, permissions granularity, discount approval/limits and audit/reasons, lifecycle/paid-order adjustments, taxes/shipping, allocation/rounding and refunds. D-043, D-044, D-045 and D-066 remain authoritative within their existing scopes. |

| D-069 | APPROVED (2026-11-14) | **Merchant-Controlled Stacking of One-Off Manual Order Discounts with Coupon Discounts (MVP).** For an order with an applied Coupon where the merchant or authorized staff wishes to add a Manual Order Discount, the DEFAULT is **NO STACKING**. The merchant or authorized staff explicitly chooses to **retain the Coupon only**, **replace the Coupon discount with the Manual Order Discount**, or **stack the two on this specific order**. Stacking is never automatic. This does not change the Coupon's original configuration or global usage conditions, does not authorize more than one coupon per order (D-061), and does not change Product/Variant catalog prices. Example: original merchandise 1,000 EGP, Coupon saving 100 EGP, manual fixed discount 50 EGP: totals 900 EGP (Coupon only), 950 EGP (Manual only), or 850 EGP (explicitly stacked), before unresolved tax/shipping effects. Keep the applied discount components separately identifiable for subsequent financial reconciliation/refunds. | Approval settles **default-off plus explicit per-order combine/replace choice** for Manual Order Discount versus Coupon. **D-070 now settles restoration if the confirmed order Coupon is actually removed/replaced by a Manual Order Discount BEFORE Preparing begins.** D-072 settles Cart-level Coupon FIRST, then Manual percentage on the remaining amount when the two are explicitly stacked. OPEN: permission to edit orders in each lifecycle stage, paid-order/payment-refund adjustment handling, authorization, audit/reasons, percent stacking order when a Cart campaign is also present, Product/Variant/Shipping Coupon interactions, taxes/shipping, rounding and proration. The capacity/hold rules D-048, D-051-D-054 remain in force for applied Coupons; D-070 is the specific pre-Preparing replacement restoration policy, not permission for general post-confirmation edits. |


| D-070 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Restore Coupon Redemption When a Confirmed Order's Applied Coupon Is Actually Replaced with a Manual Order Discount BEFORE Preparing Begins (MVP).** If an authorized merchant actor replaces/removes an applied and already-finalized Coupon discount in a confirmed order and saves a successful replacement by a Manual Order Discount **before the order enters Preparing**, restore (1) one global usage slot/capacity for that Coupon in that merchant store and (2) the original registered customer's one-use-per-coupon eligibility. Restoration is linked to the original Coupon redemption and must execute at most once; viewing an unsaved edit or merely adding a stacked manual discount does not restore the Coupon. Subsequent coupon use still must satisfy the current validity, enabled status, eligibility and global limits. | **NO automatic restoration after Preparing begins** solely because the Coupon is removed/replaced. D-053 separately handles a confirmed order cancelled before Preparing; D-069 controls merchant-selected retain/replace/stack for Manual-versus-Coupon discounts. D-070 does not approve editing confirmed orders in general, editing already paid orders, reversing captured payments, automatic adjustment of shipment/taxes, specific staff permissions, notification/approval screens, or exception handling after Preparing; those remain OPEN. Only one coupon per order is allowed under D-061. |

| D-071 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Automated Cart Discount First, Then Manual Order Discount When Explicitly Stacked (MVP).** If a merchant or authorized staff member explicitly chooses to stack an eligible Advanced automated Cart campaign with a Manual Order Discount on the same order under D-068, calculate eligible Product/Variant/Category item discounts first (D-042/D-066), then the automated Cart discount against the remaining merchandise subtotal (D-066), and LAST the Manual Order Discount against the merchandise amount remaining after those discounts. An order-level manual **percentage** discount is calculated on this final remaining amount, not on the pre-Cart subtotal. Example: merchandise after any item discounts 1,000 EGP; automated Cart discount 100 EGP; remaining 900 EGP; manual 10% = 90 EGP; illustrative after-discount merchandise total 810 EGP, before shipping/taxes. | This approves only the calculation sequence/basis for **an explicitly permitted Cart Campaign + Manual Order Discount combination**; it does not itself enable stacking (default remains OFF under D-068), authorize combining coupons or additional Cart promotions, or settle eligibility/minimum spend, simultaneous Cart campaign PLUS Coupon PLUS Manual combination (D-072 separately settles Cart-Coupon-first then Manual when no additional Cart campaign is involved), Shipping, taxes, discount proration for returns/refunds, rounding, paid-order adjustments or staff permissions. D-066 remains authoritative for item-before-Cart; D-067 remains authoritative for Manual Percentage after item discounts when Cart is absent. |


| D-072 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Cart-Level Coupon First, Then Manual Order Discount When Explicitly Combined (MVP).** If an authorized merchant actor deliberately opts to stack ONE eligible **Cart-scoped Coupon** with a Manual Order Discount on the same order under D-069, apply any eligible item-level discounts first according to D-042/D-066 (only if their independent stacking conditions allow it); next apply the Cart-level Coupon to its eligible remaining merchandise base; LAST apply the Manual Order Discount on the merchandise amount still remaining after that Coupon. For a percentage Manual Order Discount, calculate the percentage from this remaining post-Coupon amount, not the original merchandise subtotal. Example: eligible merchandise subtotal 1,000 EGP, Cart Coupon fixed saving 100 EGP, then Manual percentage 10% of remaining 900 EGP = 90 EGP; illustrative amount after discounts is **810 EGP**, before shipping and taxes. | Approval settles the **calculation sequence/basis only when Cart Coupon + Manual Discount stacking has been expressly enabled on that order**; it does not itself authorize stacking (default OFF in D-069), allow more than one Coupon (D-061), or establish stacking of the Cart Coupon with an additional automatic Cart campaign. Product/Variant-scoped Coupons, Shipping-scoped Coupons, multiple concurrent Cart campaigns, cross-scope eligibility and exclusion, tax/shipping position, fixed-discount price floors, rounding, proration/refunds, staff authorization and edits to paid orders remain OPEN. D-066, D-067, D-069 and D-071 continue to govern their separate situations. |

| D-073 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Sequential Calculation: Automated Cart Campaign First, Then Cart-Scoped Coupon When Explicitly Combined (MVP).** When an eligible automated Advanced Cart Discount Campaign and ONE eligible Cart-scoped Coupon are expressly allowed to stack under the applicable merchant-controlled combination settings (including D-062), calculate any independently permitted Product/Variant/Category item-level discounts first (D-042/D-066), next apply the automated Cart campaign to the remaining eligible merchandise subtotal, and then calculate the Cart Coupon against the amount remaining AFTER the automated Cart campaign. A percentage Coupon uses that reduced base rather than the original merchandise subtotal. Example: eligible merchandise 1,000 EGP after any permitted item-level promotions; automated Cart campaign 10% saves 100 EGP (900 EGP remaining); Cart Coupon 10% of 900 saves 90 EGP; final illustrative merchandise amount is **810 EGP**, before unresolved shipping/tax effects. | Approves **only this conditional calculation order/base**. Does NOT turn on stacking by default, grant permissions to combine discounts whose settings prohibit it (D-062), allow multiple Coupon codes (D-061), define multiple simultaneous automated Cart campaign policy, or automatically add a Manual Order Discount as a third discount. **CLOSED by D-074:** three-way Cart campaign + Cart Coupon + Manual Order Discount is permitted only with compatible merchant settings plus explicit authorized manual opt-in; calculation is Cart campaign, then Cart Coupon, then Manual. **OPEN:** Cart minimum-purchase eligibility recalculation, fixed Cart/Coupon amount capping, discount rounding and allocation, shipping/tax/refunds, staff authorization and post-payment edits. D-066, D-071 and D-072 remain authoritative for their respective cases. |

| D-074 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Explicit Three-Way Stacking: Automated Cart Campaign + ONE Cart Coupon + Manual Order Discount (MVP Advanced).** Permit all three order-level discounts on a single eligible order ONLY when the automated Cart campaign and Coupon are eligible to combine under the relevant merchant settings (D-062/D-073), AND an authorized merchant actor deliberately opts to add/stack the Manual Order Discount on that particular order (D-068/D-069). Display the individual savings, total discount and resulting order merchandise amount before the merchant confirms the explicit manual combination. **Sequential calculation:** applicable item-level discounts first if independently eligible; then automated Cart campaign; then the ONE Cart Coupon on the remaining amount (D-073); LAST the Manual Order Discount on the further-reduced merchandise amount (D-071/D-072). Example: 1,000 EGP after eligible item-level promotions, automatic Cart -10% = 900 EGP, Cart Coupon -10% of 900 = 810 EGP, Manual Order Discount -10% of 810 = **729 EGP** before shipping and taxes. | This permits the explicit **three-way combination and its order of calculation**, NOT default stacking, unilateral staff overrides of disabled campaign/coupon compatibility, multiple Coupon codes (still DEFERRED per D-061), or multiple concurrent automated Cart campaigns. Does NOT settle permission names, financial/percentage safety ceilings, approval escalation, tax/shipping interactions, Cart minimum-spend requalification, rounding, allocating discounts to items, refunds, post-payment edits, or detailed audit implementation. Those remain OPEN. |

| D-075 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Optional Merchant-Configured Maximum Combined Order-Level Discount Percentage (MVP Advanced).** The merchant can optionally enable a percentage ceiling on the **combined order-level savings** that arise from explicitly stacking an eligible Advanced automated Cart Discount Campaign, ONE eligible Cart Coupon, and an authorized Manual Order Discount (D-074). Before approving the combined discounts, display the total monetary savings and effective combined-discount percentage. When the proposed combined order-level discount percentage EXCEEDS the merchant's enabled ceiling, BLOCK approval of that stacking configuration until the discounts are revised; do not silently trim any discount or alter the customer's price. If no ceiling is configured, no extra percentage ceiling is imposed by this particular setting. | Approval applies to an optional tenant/merchant setting for **combined order-level** discounts; it does NOT change individual Product/Variant catalog prices, the independently approved item-level promotions (D-035/D-042), one-Coupon-per-order policy (D-061), or opt-in requirements for stacking (D-062/D-068/D-069/D-074). **SETTLED by D-076:** the denominator/base is the eligible merchandise subtotal AFTER independently applicable item-level discounts. **SETTLED by D-077:** the ceiling applies to any explicitly permitted stacking of TWO OR MORE order-level discounts, and does NOT constrain a standalone order-level discount by itself. **OPEN:** zero-subtotal cases, rounding, permission to edit the ceiling, whether any platform-level safeguard exists, tax/shipping/returns effects, and approval behavior for paid orders. No specific ceiling default percentage is approved. |

| D-076 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Combined Order-Level Discount Ceiling Uses Post-Item-Discount Merchandise Subtotal as Its Percentage Base (MVP Advanced).** When the merchant enables the optional D-075 ceiling for an expressly permitted stacked combination of order-level discounts, calculate the effective combined percentage as **100 × (total actual savings from the stacked order-level discounts) / (eligible merchandise subtotal AFTER applicable Product/Variant/Category item-level discounts)**. This denominator excludes shipping and taxes for the current comparison; their exact interactions remain OPEN. Example: original merchandise 1,000 EGP; item discounts 200 EGP; remaining merchandise 800 EGP; sequential order-level Cart -80, Cart Coupon -72, Manual -64.80 = 216.80 EGP combined order-level savings. Ceiling percentage = **216.80 / 800 = 27.1%**; a configured 25% ceiling BLOCKS approval of that combination until revised. | This settles the **percentage denominator** for the optional combined order-level ceiling only. It does NOT count separate item-level discounts in the ceiling numerator, authorize otherwise disallowed stacking or change the discount calculation sequence (D-066/D-071-D-074), permit negative totals, approve a default ceiling, or define zero-subtotal handling/rounding. **SETTLED by D-077:** the ceiling applies to TWO OR MORE stacked order-level discounts. **OPEN:** eligibility/base exclusions, precision and zero-denominator behavior, any platform-wide safeguard, editable permissions, taxes/shipping, returns/refunds, and paid-order modifications. |

| D-077 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Optional Combined Order-Level Discount Ceiling Applies Whenever TWO OR MORE Order-Level Discounts Are Explicitly Stacked (MVP Advanced).** If the merchant has enabled the optional combined-discount ceiling (D-075), enforce that ceiling for **any permitted combination of at least two order-level discounts**, not only the three-way Cart campaign + Cart Coupon + Manual configuration (D-074). Covered two-way cases include an eligible Automated Cart campaign + Cart Coupon, Automated Cart campaign + Manual Order Discount, or Cart Coupon + Manual Order Discount, and any approved three-way combination. Evaluate total ACTUAL savings from stacked order-level discounts as a percentage of the post-item-discount eligible merchandise subtotal per D-076, and BLOCK approval of a combination exceeding the merchant-set ceiling until it is revised; do not silently reduce discounts or reprice a customer order. | This settles **two-or-more stacked order-level discounts** versus only three. A SINGLE standalone order-level discount is **not** subjected to this particular combined-discount ceiling. Item-level promotions are NOT included in the ceiling numerator. Existing explicit stacking permissions and one-code-per-order restrictions still apply. **OPEN:** zero-subtotal/denominator handling, precise eligible merchandise exclusions, rounding, ceiling-setting permissions and audit, independent single-discount limits (especially Manual Order Discount), tax/shipping, paid-order editing, returns/refunds and other transaction-level safeguards. |

| D-078 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Manual Order Discount: Role Permission + Configurable Approval-Free Limit + Authorized Approval on Exceedance (MVP, both Basic and Advanced where Manual Order Discount is available).** Within each Merchant/Tenant, the merchant controls whether a staff role may apply one-off Manual Order Discounts and may configure a maximum percentage the role may grant without further approval. If an otherwise permitted staff member requests a Manual Order Discount exceeding that role's configured approval-free limit, the operation requires approval by an appropriately authorized manager/store owner before it can be applied. The approval pathway must NOT bypass an enabled combined-order-discount ceiling or the stacking and order-state restrictions already approved (D-044, D-068-D-077). Platform feature entitlements, merchant configuration, and staff permission/approval are distinct controls. | This settles the **existence** of role-based discount permission, optional approval-free percentage thresholds, and escalation when exceeding an applicable configured threshold; it does NOT approve example thresholds (10%/20%), exact roles/permission keys, approval UX is now SETTLED by D-079 (in-app request reviewed in an authorized manager account, no same-device PIN required for MVP); approver approval ceilings/escalation (dedicated approver permission and prohibition of self-approval SETTLED by D-084), expiry of requests, who may configure/override role limits, how a fixed-amount discount is compared with a percentage limit, per-order vs per-staff caps, reason/audit fields, stage restrictions, approval after payment, or a global single-discount ceiling. Those remain OPEN. Approval may NOT override the hard enabled D-075-D-077 combined-discount ceiling. |

| D-079 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **In-App Manager Approval for Manual Order Discounts Exceeding Role Limits (MVP; Basic and Advanced).** If a staff member with Manual Order Discount permission requests an exception above that role's configured approval-free percentage limit (D-078), the employee submits an approval request within the app, associated with the relevant order and proposed discount. An appropriately authorized manager/store owner can review and explicitly APPROVE or REJECT the request from their OWN signed-in account within the platform, including remotely without being at the staff member's device. Same-device manager PIN is NOT required for the MVP-approved process. An over-limit discount must not become effective merely because the request is submitted; it needs authorized approval and must obey existing stacking rules and any active hard combined-discount ceiling (D-075-D-077). | Approval covers **in-app request and manager decision**, not a separate manager-PIN approval workflow. **SETTLED by D-080:** for an order/draft awaiting approval BEFORE finalization, hold order finalization and collection at the proposed discounted price. **OPEN:** behavior for already-confirmed or paid orders; request notifications, timeouts, cancellation/editing, duplicate requests, approver-level financial limits/escalation (permission and no self-approval SETTLED by D-084), exact required explanation/audit fields, the approval screen, and the rule for comparing Fixed Amount discounts against percentage role thresholds. No discount percentage defaults or role list are approved. |

| D-080 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Hold Order Finalization While Over-Limit Manual Discount Awaits In-App Approval (MVP).** For a not-yet-finalized customer order or draft where a staff member has requested a Manual Order Discount above their approval-free role limit (D-078/D-079), preserve the order/draft and the associated pending manager-approval request. Until an authorized manager/owner has decided, do **NOT** finalize/confirm the order at the proposed discounted amount and do **NOT** collect payment at that proposed discounted amount. The pending approval is tied to that order; the employee can work on other orders while waiting. An approval request by itself does not apply the discount or bypass existing stacking restrictions or active hard combined-discount ceilings (D-075-D-077). | Approved ONLY for orders not yet confirmed/paid, and for holding finalization and collection at the unapproved price. D-081 NOW SETTLES rejection for not-yet-finalized orders: keep draft, allow removal/reduction/revision and a new approval request, and require customer acceptance of the final total. Manager-APPROVAL aftermath is SETTLED by D-082 (employee must finalize the draft separately after customer agreement; approval does not auto-confirm or charge). Separate approval status UX, stock reservation or customer visibility while waiting, request expiry/reminders, escalation, post-approval draft edits (settled by D-083 for financial-impact changes), already confirmed/paid order modifications, and detailed employee draft-edit permissions remain OPEN. No automatic approval/confirmation is implied. |

| D-081 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Manager Rejection of an Over-Limit Manual Discount: Keep Order Draft and Allow Revision / Resubmission (MVP).** If an authorized approver REJECTS an employee's pending in-app Manual Order Discount exception request (D-078/D-079/D-080), KEEP the associated order as an unfinalized draft: do not automatically cancel the order, confirm it, or apply the rejected price. The employee may (a) remove the proposed discount, (b) lower the requested discount to a level the employee is otherwise authorized to grant without separate approval, or (c) revise it to a new over-limit amount and submit a NEW approval request. The customer must agree to the final total before the order is finalized. Rejection of the discount is not rejection of the sale itself. | Approved for **rejected exception requests on not-yet-finalized orders/drafts only**. The rejected discount must not be applied or collected. Whether a manager may suggest an alternative amount, whether previous approval requests are preserved in a particular audit-log structure, explanation/reason requirements, notifying employees/customers, who may revise a draft, resubmission limits/expiry, implications for coupon holds/stock reservations, and treatment of confirmed or paid orders remain OPEN. **SETTLED by D-082:** manager approval authorizes the requested draft discount but does not auto-finalize or collect payment; the employee must review the final total with the customer and explicitly finalize. OPEN: how subsequent draft edits affect that approval. |

| D-082 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Manager Approval Enables the Proposed Manual Discount on the Draft, but Does NOT Finalize the Order (MVP).** When an authorized manager/store owner APPROVES an employee's over-limit Manual Order Discount request (D-078/D-079), that approval permits the specifically requested discount to be applied to the existing unfinalized order/draft. The employee must explicitly review the resulting total and remaining order details with the customer, obtain customer agreement to the final price, and deliberately complete/finalize the order as a separate action. Approval alone never auto-confirms the order, collects payment, starts fulfillment, or bypasses already-approved inventory, payment, discount-stacking, and ceiling rules. | This settles **manager-approval aftermath for unfinalized drafts only**, paired with D-081 (rejection). It does NOT authorize changes to already-confirmed or paid orders. **SETTLED by D-083:** financial draft edits affecting total or discount invalidate the earlier approval and need new approval when still above the staff limit; non-financial edits alone do not. **OPEN:** staff notification, approval expiry, audit details, draft stock/coupon hold effects, and exact finalization workflow. |

| D-083 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Financial Order/Draft Changes After Manager Approval Invalidate the Previous Over-Limit Manual Discount Approval (MVP).** An approval granted under D-079/D-082 is valid for the specific financial order/draft context presented to the approver. If staff subsequently edits items, quantities, selling prices, applicable discounts, shipping fee, or any other field that changes the order total OR the effective discount value, the prior approval can no longer authorize finalizing the altered draft at the previously approved exception. Recalculate the draft; if the resulting manual discount remains ABOVE the staff member's configured approval-free role limit under D-078, a NEW in-app manager approval is required before finalization. Edits that do NOT affect the financial totals or discount amount (for example, a non-financial note) do NOT, by themselves, require renewed discount approval. Customer acceptance of the final price and separate employee finalization remain required by D-082. | This approval settles **re-approval after financial edits to an unfinalized draft** only; no old approval may silently authorize a financially changed draft. It does not define broader edit permissions or automatically allow editing every field, does not authorize edits after payment or order confirmation, and does not decide detailed audit/history presentation, notifications, approval expiration, the precise set of financial-impact fields, currency/tax rounding, or stock/coupon-hold recalculation. If a revised discount is at or below the employee's authorized limit, ordinary staff permission and other established discount/order rules apply without requiring the old exceptional approval. |

| D-084 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Dedicated Approval Permission for Over-Limit Manual Order Discounts (MVP).** Each merchant/store owner can assign a dedicated **Approve Manual Discounts** permission to an appropriate role/user (for example a manager or supervisor), independently of general manager role membership. Only an actor holding the required approval authorization may decide an employee's over-limit in-app manual-discount request under D-079; being labeled Manager alone is not sufficient without the relevant permission. **Self-approval is prohibited:** the staff member who submitted an exception request cannot approve that same request, even if they otherwise hold the approval permission. If a user is already authorized to grant the requested discount within their own approval-free permission/limit, they do not have to submit an exception request to themselves. | This settles **delegable dedicated approval authority and no self-approval**, rather than Manager-role-only approval or same-device PIN. It does NOT specify role names or permission keys. **D-085 now approves optional maximum approval percentages per authorized role**; how Fixed Amount requests compare with those limits, exact escalation routing, whether multiple approvers can act, branch/location boundaries, notification/audit requirements, and approvals for already-confirmed/paid orders remain OPEN. D-078 permission/threshold rules, D-080 draft hold, D-082 separate employee finalization, D-083 financial-change reapproval, and hard D-075-D-077 combined ceiling remain in force. |

| D-085 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Optional Role-Based Maximum Approval Percentage for Exceptional Manual Order Discounts (MVP; Basic and Advanced).** The merchant/store owner may configure, for each role granted the dedicated **Approve Manual Discounts** permission (D-084), an OPTIONAL maximum discount percentage that role can approve for OTHER staff members. If an exception request exceeds the approver role's configured ceiling, that role cannot approve it; it requires an appropriately authorized approver with a higher permitted ceiling. This **approval ceiling is DISTINCT** from the maximum manual discount the role may itself grant without approval under D-078. An approver must have the dedicated approval permission, may not approve their own request (D-084), and cannot override an enabled hard combined-discount ceiling (D-075-D-077). Example ONLY, not defaults: sales staff 10% grant/no approval, supervisor 20% grant / up to 30% approve, manager 30% grant / up to 50% approve. | Approval covers **role-configurable optional approval-percentage ceilings** and the rule that requests exceeding the particular approver's allowed ceiling need an approver with higher authority. It does **NOT** set default percentages/role names or a maximum platform percentage. **SETTLED by D-086:** fixed-amount Manual Order Discounts are converted to an effective percentage using the eligible merchandise amount remaining after previous permitted discounts and immediately before the manual discount. **SETTLED by D-087: shipping fees are excluded from the manual discount and its percentage base. SETTLED by D-088: taxes are likewise excluded. OPEN:** tax-inclusive price handling, zero-base behavior, rounding/precision, handling multiple roles/overlapping authorizations, who can change these limits, precise escalation routing/notifications/approval timeouts, case of no suitable approver, branch/location scope, audit details, and requests on confirmed/paid orders. D-079-D-083 draft approval flow remains applicable. |

| D-086 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Fixed-Amount Manual Order Discount: Effective Percentage Uses Remaining Merchandise Amount (MVP; Basic and Advanced).** To evaluate a fixed-amount Manual Order Discount against the employee's discount-without-approval percentage limit (D-078) or the approver role's maximum approval percentage (D-085), convert the requested FIXED monetary discount to an effective percentage: **fixed discount amount / eligible merchandise amount remaining immediately BEFORE applying that manual discount × 100**. This base is the merchandise amount after earlier discounts that are explicitly permitted to stack, not the original undiscounted total. Example: original item total 1,000 EGP, prior eligible discounts 300 EGP, remaining before manual discount 700 EGP, proposed manual fixed discount 150 EGP -> **21.43%**. A supervisor with a 20% approval ceiling cannot authorize this request; a suitably authorized higher-level approver is required. | Settles the percentage comparison BASE for fixed-amount manual discounts only, and must follow previously approved stacking/order rules (D-066, D-071-D-074). Does NOT approve new default limits or roles, force combination of discounts, authorize negative totals, override hard combined ceiling (D-075-D-077), or change previously approved rules for payment and fulfillment. **SETTLED by D-087 and D-088: shipping and tax are excluded from the eligible manual-discount and effective-percentage base. OPEN:** detailed treatment of tax-inclusive/tax-exclusive prices, how to handle a zero remaining base, rounding/precision and monetary allocation, mixed Basic Services, and detailed notification/escalation workflow. |

| D-087 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Manual Order Discount Excludes Shipping from the Discount Base and Staff/Approver Percentage Limits (MVP; Basic and Advanced).** The one-off Manual Order Discount approved in D-043 targets the eligible MERCHANDISE amount only, not the delivery/shipping charge. For a Percentage Manual Order Discount, do not include a separate shipping fee in the percentage base. For a Fixed Amount Manual Order Discount, when converting to an effective percentage under D-086 to compare against staff approval-free limits (D-078) and approver ceilings (D-085), the denominator excludes shipping and uses eligible merchandise after earlier permitted discounts, immediately before manual discount. Example: eligible remaining merchandise 700 EGP, shipping 80 EGP, manual fixed discount 70 EGP -> 10% (70 / 700), NOT ~8.97% (70 / 780). Shipping Discount and Shipping Fee Override remain separate from this Manual Order Discount and under their own approved or open rules. | Settles exclusion of SHIPPING from the Manual Order Discount eligible amount and percentage-limit calculations; does not settle tax-inclusive versus tax-exclusive merchandise calculations, tax recalculation, whether shipping can receive a separate authorized reduction, zero merchandise subtotals, precision/rounding, mixed-service eligibility, paid-order edits, refund allocation, or other channel-specific rules. Existing financial-change reapproval D-083 still applies if altering shipping changes the order total, and D-075-D-077 ceilings and D-084 no self-approval remain in force. |

| D-088 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Exclude Tax from the Manual Order Discount Calculation Base and Staff/Approver Percentage Limits (MVP; Basic and Advanced).** The eligible base for a percentage Manual Order Discount and the denominator used to convert a Fixed Amount Manual Order Discount into its effective percentage for D-078/D-085 permission limits must EXCLUDE tax, as well as separate shipping fees already excluded by D-087. For tax-exclusive merchandise amounts, use the eligible post-prior-discount merchandise amount before tax. Example: eligible merchandise 700 EGP before 14% tax (98 EGP), shipping 80 EGP, proposed manual discount 70 EGP -> permission comparison 70/700 = **10%**, not 70/798. Taxes payable must be calculated/recalculated consistently with the applicable tax treatment after eligible discounts, without silently changing customer totals. | Approval settles **tax exclusion from manual-discount and role-limit percentage bases** only. It does NOT determine precise tax-inclusive catalog price extraction, how to display the customer-facing inclusive discount, jurisdiction-specific tax calculation, tax on shipping, inclusive/exclusive rounding, multi-rate carts, zero eligible base, refund tax adjustments or taxes for Basic Services; these remain OPEN. It does not grant permission for a tax-inclusive amount to bypass staff limits, or change D-066-D-087 approved discount sequences. |

| D-089 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **MVP Supports BOTH Tax-Inclusive and Tax-Exclusive Merchandise Pricing.** A merchant may work with prices entered inclusive of applicable tax or entered before tax. For a tax-INCLUSIVE selling price, the tax portion is already included in the price shown to the customer; the platform must distinguish the underlying merchandise value and embedded tax without adding the same tax again. For a tax-EXCLUSIVE selling price, the platform calculates/adds applicable tax separately. These supported price models apply to catalog Products and independently priced Variants, and must remain consistent with the approved D-088 rule that tax is excluded from the Manual Order Discount base and role-approval percentage comparisons. Example ONLY (single hypothetical 14% tax, no other adjustments): 100 EGP net + 14 EGP tax = 114 EGP gross; a merchant can enter 114 as tax-inclusive or 100 as tax-exclusive, with a 114 gross price in either scenario. | This approves **support for both inclusive/exclusive price input/treatment in MVP**, with store-vs-market input-mode choice now SETTLED by D-090 (one store default in MVP; future market overrides deferred); product-specific tax eligibility remains OPEN, tax registration or jurisdiction rates, tax-liability rules, tax categories/exemptions, service taxation, shipping taxation, multi-rate/multi-jurisdiction carts, discount allocation, exact rounding/precision and invoice display, taxes on returns/refunds, changing a store's tax mode after sales, or a full multi-market pricing feature. Those remain OPEN. Preserve tax/shipping exclusions for Manual Order Discount per D-087/D-088; do not retroactively reprice already agreed orders. |
| D-090 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) / DEFERRED per-market override | **Store Default + Future Market Overrides for Tax Pricing Input Mode.** In MVP, each Merchant store / Tenant has ONE default setting selecting whether merchandise prices are entered **Tax-Inclusive** or **Tax-Exclusive**. This store-wide mode applies to catalog Products and independently priced Variants; it determines price entry interpretation, NOT whether an individual item is legally taxable. **Per-Market override of the inclusive/exclusive input mode is DEFERRED** to a later multi-market capability discussion. The merchant configures this permitted store setting within the store. | This approves the store-level price-input-mode scope in MVP and postpones market-specific mode overrides; it does NOT defer or approve the full multi-market feature. It does NOT decide the default initial selection, whether/how merchants may change modes after sales, customer-facing market-dependent price presentation, tax registration, rates, per-item taxable/exempt/zero-rated treatment, tax categories, services/shipping tax, jurisdiction calculations, precision/rounding, invoice display, historical order snapshots, or refund tax adjustments. D-089 support of BOTH price models and D-088 exclusion of tax from Manual Order Discount/approval percentage bases remain intact. |
| D-091 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Store Default + Product Tax Status / Tax Profile Override (MVP).** Individual Products inherit the store default tax treatment unless the merchant selects a different product-level treatment. Support clearly distinct **Taxable**, **Exempt**, and **Zero-Rated** statuses. A taxable Product may be assigned a different reusable **Tax Profile** (including a different applicable tax rate) from the store default, rather than manually typing a free-form tax percentage on each Product. Preserve historical confirmed-order/invoice tax details when subsequent catalog tax settings change. | Approval is for per-Product tax treatment and reusable Tax Profiles in MVP, not a complete jurisdiction-specific tax engine. **SETTLED by D-093:** merchant creation/management with separate staff permission. **OPEN:** edit-in-use behavior, allowed rates and categories, rate validation, exemption/zero-rating reason requirements, profile changes after sales, tax-inclusive extraction and rounding, shipping/service taxation, invoices/refunds, exact historical snapshot mechanics, and multi-market overrides (the price-input-mode override is DEFERRED by D-090). SETTLED by D-092: all Variants inherit their parent Product Tax Profile/treatment in MVP; independent per-Variant tax overrides are not supported in MVP. |
| D-092 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Product-Level Tax Profile Inheritance for All Variants (MVP).** Every sellable Variant of a Variable Product inherits the parent Product's tax status and assigned Tax Profile (or its inherited store default), regardless of the Variant's independent selling price under D-035. Changing the Product's tax treatment affects the calculation for all its Variants on future sales. **No Per-Variant Tax Profile/Tax Rate Override is available in MVP.** When legally different tax treatment is necessary for a specific offering, the merchant should list it as a separate Product with the appropriate Product-level Tax Profile. Historical confirmed orders/invoices retain their original tax details according to D-091. | Settles ONLY Product-versus-Variant tax inheritance and intentionally excludes individual Variant tax overrides in MVP. Does NOT set legal tax rates, tax registration/compliance, Tax Profile editing-in-use behavior or validation (authority SETTLED by D-093), Basic Service taxation, mixed-tax carts, taxes on shipping, changes to existing confirmed/paid orders, inclusive/exclusive rounding, or refund adjustments. **Future Per-Variant Tax Overrides are not approved**, and may be reconsidered in later discovery if a justified business case appears. |

| D-093 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Merchant-Managed Tax Profiles (MVP).** Each Tenant/Merchant may CREATE and MANAGE reusable Tax Profiles in that store's tax settings, including name, applicable tax rate and tax-treatment/status, and assign an appropriate available profile to Products. A Product inherits the store default unless explicitly assigned another profile (D-091). Management of Tax Profiles is guarded by a separately assignable staff permission. Variants continue to inherit their Product's profile (D-092). Previously confirmed order and invoice tax details remain historical and are not changed merely by catalog/profile updates. | This approves merchant authority and reusable per-store profiles, **not** unrestricted legal validity of arbitrary tax rates or fully automatic compliance. **SETTLED by D-094:** an in-use Tax Profile can have its rate updated with affected-Product count, explicit confirmation and audit, applying to future sales without changing confirmed history. **OPEN:** pending-order impacts, rate-change effective-date scheduling, deactivation/deletion, allowed rates/validations, proof/reasons for Exempt/Zero-Rated treatments, tax on Basic Services and shipping, inclusive-price handling when rates change, rounding, invoice/credit/refund consequences, and jurisdiction-specific requirements. Super Admin feature entitlements remain separate from merchant-side tax permissions; no unrestricted per-Variant override (D-092). |
| D-094 | APPROVED (2026-11-15) | **Update an In-Use Merchant Tax Profile with Explicit Confirmation (MVP).** An authorized merchant may edit the tax RATE of an existing Tax Profile even when it is already assigned to Products. Before saving, the system shows a clear warning, the previous and proposed rate, and the count of currently assigned Products potentially affected, and requires explicit confirmation. The revised rate is used by those assigned Products/Variants for **NEW SALES** after the change. Log the actor, time, old rate and new rate. Prior confirmed orders/invoices remain as originally recorded and must not be retroactively repriced/re-taxed merely by this profile update. | This chooses UPDATE EXISTING rather than requiring a new Profile plus reassignment. Does NOT settle taxation of pending/unconfirmed orders or already paid orders, effective-date scheduling, rate-validation/legal correctness, whether a tax-inclusive display price is preserved or its taxable base is reinterpreted on a rate change, or permissions beyond already approved independent Tax Profile management permission (D-093). Deactivation/deletion and historical tax snapshots/refunds remain OPEN. |

## Catalog module discussion candidates - NOT yet approved

The following are PROPOSED feature boundaries for discovery, not engineering modules or Spec Kit deliverables:
1. Product and Basic Service Management.
2. Attributes & Variants.
3. Categories.
4. Pricing.
5. Discounts and Promotions.
6. Catalog Publication / Visibility.

Suggested distinctions - all PROPOSED:
- **Descriptive vs. Variant-defining use is APPROVED at the product level (D-031).** An Attribute reused by a merchant can be configured for description/display on one product and for Variants on another. Examples such as material or color are illustrative, not fixed mappings.
- **APPROVED by D-032:** For Variant-defining Attributes selected on a product, the merchant sees automatically generated combinations and chooses which are offered for sale; unselected combinations are not purchasable.
- **APPROVED by D-033:** System-generated SKU for each offered Variant, with merchant override provided it remains unique within that Tenant/store.
- **PROPOSED details:** SKU formatting and change restrictions, per-Variant quantity rules, image rules, and editing behavior remain OPEN. **APPROVED by D-034 and D-035:** a Variable Product has a base selling price; new Variants receive a price COPY at creation, and all Variant prices remain independently editable. Changing the base price never updates existing Variants automatically; explicit Apply to All / Apply to Selected bulk actions are available, with a simple overwrite warning.
- **Customer-provided Dynamic Service Fields:** Data such as print dimensions, text, page count, or document upload; reserved for a future Advanced Service feature, NOT a Product Variant in MVP.
- A product's historical sales/order details and agreed price should remain meaningful after later catalog changes; precise edit/delete rules are open.
- Basic Service ordinarily does not participate in warehouse stock, reservation or dispatch as a physical good; this is a proposal awaiting confirmation of the exact commercial fulfillment flow.

## Discount Engine - approved levels and scopes / open mechanics (2026-11-13)

**APPROVED D-079 (2026-11-15):** Over-limit Manual Order Discounts require an in-app approval request and explicit authorized manager/store-owner decision from their own account, including remote approval. **APPROVED D-084:** a separately delegable Approve Manual Discounts permission controls who may approve; a requester cannot approve their own exception request. **APPROVED D-085:** authorized approver roles may have separate optional maximum approval percentages. **APPROVED D-086:** fixed manual discount amounts are compared with these role limits using their effective percentage of the eligible post-prior-discount amount immediately before the manual discount. Same-device PIN is NOT the chosen MVP workflow. **APPROVED D-080:** For not-yet-finalized orders, hold order finalization and payment collection at the proposed discounted total while awaiting approval; the order/draft and request remain available, and staff may process other orders. The discount is NOT applied merely by requesting approval. D-081 settles rejected-request behavior (draft remains editable and can be re-submitted; no automatic cancellation/confirmation); D-082 settles APPROVED-request behavior (discount may be applied to draft, then employee/customer review and explicit finalization; no auto-confirmation). Already-confirmed/paid-order effects remain OPEN; financial edits after approval are SETTLED by D-083.


**APPROVED D-036:** Basic and Advanced Discount levels are in MVP, with individual Tenant availability controlled by Platform Super Admin and merchant-side configuration limited by entitlements. **DEFERRED:** Professional Promotions. Defining feature levels does not authorize every example previously floated for a given level.

**Approved target-scope split (D-037):** Basic can target **Products and Variants**. Advanced additionally can target **Categories, Cart, and Shipping**. This approval does not settle discount formulas, conditions, or application style.

**APPROVED D-038:** A parent-Product discount on a Variable Product covers its offered Variants dynamically, including offered Variants added during the campaign; a selected-Variant discount affects only its explicitly targeted Variants. Variant selling prices remain independent and are not changed by application of a discount. Merchant notifications about inclusion of new Variants are a proposed UX detail, not separately approved.

**APPROVED D-039:** Product- and selected-Variant-scoped Basic Discounts support both **Percentage (%)** and **Fixed Amount** without changing stored Variant selling prices. **APPROVED D-040:** For these Product/Variant fixed-amount discounts, the specified amount applies **per eligible purchased unit**, not once per order line. **APPROVED D-041:** If the configured fixed amount exceeds a unit's selling price, cap its actual discount at that price (final unit amount cannot go negative); warn the merchant and require explicit confirmation before activating a campaign that makes any currently targeted offered item free. **APPROVED D-042:** Among competing Product-, Variant- and Category-scoped item discounts that apply to the same unit, use the ONE producing the greatest actual saving, with no item-level stacking. Cart/Coupon/Shipping combinations remain OPEN.

**APPROVED D-043:** **Manual Order Discount** is a one-off discount applied manually to one specific customer order, with Percentage or Fixed Amount as choices, and is available to tenants with either **Basic** or **Advanced** entitlement. It is separate from reusable/automatic **Cart Discount Campaigns**, which are **Advanced only**. A manual order discount does not create a campaign, permanently change customer prices, or modify product/Variant catalog prices. Staff permissions, mandatory reasons/audit, edit timing, eligible amount, interactions with Cart/Coupon campaigns, and refund calculations remain OPEN.

**APPROVED D-044:** **Manual Order Discount versus existing automatic item promotions uses Merchant-Controlled Stacking.** Default: do not stack. An authorized actor explicitly chooses to REPLACE automatic item promotions for that order or ADD the manual discount on top of them. This explicit opt-in to combining is supplemented by D-067, which settles the manual percentage-after-item calculation basis only. Eligibility boundaries, maximum limits, staff permission granularity, audit, refunds, and whether automated Cart campaigns / Coupons can stack with a manual discount remain OPEN.

**APPROVED D-067:** If a one-off manual percentage discount is explicitly stacked with item-level Product/Variant/Category discounts, calculate that percentage on the discounted merchandise subtotal remaining after those item-level discounts, not on the original subtotal. The example 1,000 EGP merchandise minus 100 EGP item discounts leaves 900 EGP; manual 10% saves 90 EGP, producing 810 EGP before unresolved shipping/taxes. **D-068 separately settles whether manual and automated Cart discounts may stack (default NO; explicit opt-in). OPEN:** manual-versus-Coupon stacking, multi-order-level percentage ordering/base when Cart+Manual combine, fixed-manual-amount limits, staff permissions, paid-order edits, tax/shipping, and refund allocation.
**APPROVED D-068:** On an order with an eligible Advanced automated Cart campaign, a one-off Manual Order Discount does **not** combine with the Cart campaign by default. Merchant/authorized staff may explicitly keep the Cart campaign only, replace it with the Manual discount, or combine both **for that order only**. The fixed-manual-discount example is 1,000 EGP before discount, Cart campaign 10% (-100 EGP), manual fixed discount 50 EGP: 900 / 950 / 850 EGP respectively. **OPEN:** sequence/basis for stacked percentage Manual discounts when a Cart campaign also exists, manual-versus-Coupon, exact staff authorization, audit, taxes/shipping, and paid-order/refund adjustments.
